L // Unmarshal parses the JSON-encoded data and stores the result
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 63 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // in the value pointed to by v. If v is nil or not a pointer,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 62 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // Unmarshal returns an [InvalidUnmarshalError].
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 48 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // Unmarshal uses the inverse of the encodings that
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 51 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // [Marshal] uses, allocating maps, slices, and pointers as necessary,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 70 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // with the following additional rules:
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 39 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal JSON into a pointer, Unmarshal first handles the case of
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 72 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // the JSON being the JSON literal null. In that case, Unmarshal sets
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 69 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // the pointer to nil. Otherwise, Unmarshal unmarshals the JSON into
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 68 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // the value pointed at by the pointer. If the pointer is nil, Unmarshal
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 72 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // allocates a new value for it to point to.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 44 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal JSON into a value implementing [Unmarshaler],
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 61 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // Unmarshal calls that value's [Unmarshaler.UnmarshalJSON] method, including
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 77 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // when the input is a JSON null.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 33 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // Otherwise, if the value implements [encoding.TextUnmarshaler]
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 64 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // and the input is a JSON quoted string, Unmarshal calls
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 57 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // [encoding.TextUnmarshaler.UnmarshalText] with the unquoted form of the string.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 81 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal JSON into a struct, Unmarshal matches incoming object
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 69 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // keys to the keys used by [Marshal] (either the struct field name or its tag),
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 80 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // preferring an exact match but also accepting a case-insensitive match.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 73 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // If a name matches multiple fields, the field whose name matches exactly
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 74 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // is chosen. By default, object keys which don't have a corresponding
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 70 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // struct field are ignored (see [Decoder.DisallowUnknownFields] for an alternative).
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 85 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // Incoming object members are processed in the order they are observed.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 72 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // If an object includes duplicate names, later values will replace or be
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 73 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // merged into prior values, depending on the Go value type.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 60 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // Case-insensitive matching provides another vector through which
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 66 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // duplicate names can occur: for example, the names "foo" and "Foo"
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 68 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // may both match the same Go struct field.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 43 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal JSON into an interface value,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 45 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // Unmarshal stores one of these in the interface value:
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 56 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L //   - bool, for JSON booleans
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 30 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //   - float64, for JSON numbers
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 32 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //   - string, for JSON strings
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 31 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //   - []any, for JSON arrays
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 29 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //   - map[string]any, for JSON objects
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 39 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //   - nil for JSON null
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 24 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal a JSON array into a slice, Unmarshal decodes each JSON array
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 76 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // element into the corresponding slice element, reusing existing slice
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 71 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // elements in-place. The slice grows to accommodate additional elements,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 73 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // or is truncated if the JSON array is shorter.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 48 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // As a special case, to unmarshal an empty JSON array into a slice,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 68 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // Unmarshal replaces the slice with a new empty slice.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 55 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal a JSON array into a Go array, Unmarshal decodes
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 63 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // JSON array elements into corresponding Go array elements.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 60 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // If the Go array is smaller than the JSON array,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 50 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // the additional JSON array elements are discarded.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 52 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // If the JSON array is smaller than the Go array,
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 50 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // the additional Go array elements are set to zero values.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 59 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // To unmarshal a JSON object into a map, Unmarshal first establishes a map to
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 78 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // use. If the map is nil, Unmarshal allocates a new map. Otherwise Unmarshal
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 77 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // reuses the existing map, keeping existing entries. Unmarshal then stores
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 75 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 77 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 82 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
L // If the JSON-encoded data contain a syntax error, Unmarshal returns a [SyntaxError].
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 86 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 62 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 66 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 63 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 69 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 68 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L // the problematic one will be unmarshaled into the target object.
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
T 66 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go
L //
T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go

T 2 source.go comment.line.double-slash.go punctuation.definition.comment.go
